2026-10-25 20:13 PDT CLOCK-RESET -> PATH-HBM/RESET (q_hub_nw G2 FF hold -2.93 / 1260 EP + 37 slew >320, max 538): diagnosed from edge_1025/g2/part_q_hub_nw (ff_hold.rpt, insts.tsv, drv.rpt). (a) hold: every failing pair is relay->relay with 0.03 ps wire: reset_tree 'pads at the taps' STACKS the depth-K pad relays on adjacent rows at the tap (u_rrl_1502/1503/1504 at 6147-6148/899.9-900.5; u_rrl_114/115/116 at 1784/5.1-5.7): a zero-logic shift chain of DFFHQNx1 (clk->q 30.2 FF) vs hold 8.1 + 25 uncertainty = -2.9 by construction, on every tap of every type. (b) slew: each 405-um hop is driven by the bare DFFHQNx1 QN (wire*/A and u_rrl D at 350-538). STRUCTURAL FIX (one cell + one planner rule, all dies): (1) ot_dp_rrelay = flop + a kept output driver (BUFx4f-class, keep/dont_touch, in gen_rtl CELLS and the g2 model): drives the 405-um hop inside 320 ps and adds ~10 ps FF to every relay->relay path, so a co-located pad pair has clk->q+buf ~40 > 33 required; (2) reset_tree: spread the pad stages along the tap's own incoming L path (equal segments <= relay_um) instead of stacking them at the tap, stack only the remainder when the path is shorter than K x ~20 um. Both are latency-neutral (depth K unchanged). Not ECO/hold buffers. Needs a RESET plan delta (gen_rtl CELLS change regenerates every kit's relay cell) -> COORD batch; CLOCK-RESET has NOT implemented it this session.

2026-10-26 01:09 PDT PATH-HBM: CUT LOWER BOUND + GRT VARIANTS (owner question "are the HBM overflows fundamental?"). ANSWER: NO, the floorplan is not the blocker. The blocker is the route ceiling declared on the hard cores.
- Bound method: epyc1:/srv/opentallas-scratch2/claude/path-hbm/batch_2330/cutbound/cutbound.py, run with the plan-era tools 82e095df4.
  - Every vertical and horizontal cut, at a 125-um step, split into per-partition segments.
  - Demand: the bits of the planned straight port-to-port lane segments crossing the cut (jogs never cross a perpendicular cut).
  - Capacity: the partition FT tracks on the allowed layers (H M4+M6 / V M5+M7; FT <= M7, so M8/M9 add 0).
    - Inside a die_ft_band, the share is the band's die_share.
    - Elsewhere it is ft_track_frac 0.25; the other 75% stays with the tile's own logic.
    - A layer is worth 0 wherever a planned hard core's OBS covers it.
  - No GRT capacity-model artefact is involved.
- Plan B1 (5fa3c6e64838) with the cores' declared ceilings (262 cores at M6/M7):
  - Worst full cut 0.415: V@13.6 mm through root, 9,596 b / 23,105 tracks.
  - Partition segments with V-cuts (H wires): 6 above 0.55, 6 above 0.60, 0 above 1.0. Worst: NW/NE_b110 lower row 1,262 b (L48 634 + L53 612) on 2,005 tracks = 0.63, because the M7-walled cores leave 0.06 of no-OBS capacity unused. Next: b100 0.48 (L5 2,725 b) and root 0.42.
  - Partition segments with H-cuts (V wires): 18 above 0.55, 4 above 0.60, 0 above 1.0. Worst: NW/NE_spu1 H@5.9 mm 8,359 b / 13,387 = 0.62 (L2 2,999 + L33 + L40; no cores, so this is pure FT share). Then the 380-um bank columns b002/b011, each carrying one 2,068-b lane, at 0.597.
- Same plan with core ceilings at M5 (pins M4/M5, OBS M1-M5):
  - V-cuts: 0 above 0.55. Worst spu1 0.395, su21 0.372.
  - H-cuts: unchanged, 4 above 0.60 (spu1 0.62, b002/b011 0.60). These are small FT-share items: widen the spu1 NS band, or give the 2,068-b bank-column lanes a die_ft_band.
- Die GRT probes (DIE-PROTO die_trial, fixed capacity model, results under epyc1:/srv/opentallas-scratch2/claude/path-hbm/batch_2330/grt_*):
  - B1 as declared, 5 it: 1,729,904 with 17,674 zero-capacity gcells.

  - All cores at M4: 0 already at 5 it.
  - Only the 221 non-root cores at M5 (root's 41 kept at M6/M7), 50 it: 1,196,448 (M7 1.01M / M6 183k).
  - Only root at M5: 50-it run in progress.
  - So the 41 root cores are decisive: 32 TKM tiles (tile_core__idpin__strap x31 + terminal x1) declared M6, plus tkm_m root, hc_root tails, idx_topk_link, idx_rootctl, argmax_vm_r2 and cfg_collar, each M6/M7. The L4/L4m/L9 root FT runs over them.

  - Input: per hub 3,024 stations, 301.6 kb, 2.23 M bit-regs on 10,686 distinct 8.64-um reg sites (bits/site median 36, p90 527, max 8,224).

  - NW hub: 6,611 banks (1,095 x 1024 + 5,516 x 256), 2.15 M bits banked; W/E halves **174k / 244k** cells. NE hub: 6,740 banks (1,151 + 5,589), halves **174k / 223k**. Both under 360k; 2 types <= 8, so (B) passes COORD's criteria, no fallback to (A).
  - Pins per bank: 1024 b -> 2,049 (D, Q, clk); 256 b -> 513. At 6 pins/um per face, banks are pin-bound strips: in on one long side, out on the other, 171 x ~5 um (1024) and 43 x ~5 um (256). They fit 17-um streets. Capacity use 82 % (padding).
  - Range scanned: 2-4 types, threshold 64-256 b, bins 8.64/34.56. Threshold 256 b on 8.64 bins gives halves 385-412k = FAIL, so the 34.56-um bin matters.
  - Costs and risks:
    - ~13.4k new hard-core instances die-wide (today 1,063 placements). Each is a clock sink with an early tap from the bank's signed view; plan-fixed placement only, no macro placer.
    - bind.py emits stations as ot_dp_st cells. Emitting them as bank instances is a bind change (shared tool -> ROOT-MERGE) or a die-local post-bind rewrite. Lane default: die-local post-bind rewrite.
